      公司簡介
      COMPANY PROFILE
      華潤萬象生活有限公司（簡稱：華潤萬象生活，           China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited (“CR Mixc
      股份代號：01209.HK）是中國領先的物業管理及       Lifestyle”) (stock code: 01209.HK) is the leading property
      商業運營服務商，2020 年 12 月 9 日於港交所上    management and commerci al operati onal serv ices
      市，2022年11月18日獲納入香港恒生指數成份        provider in China. It was listed on the Hong Kong Stock
      股。公司以「城市品質生活服務平台」為戰略定           Exchange on 9 December 2020, and was included in the
      位，堅持圍繞「成為客戶信賴和喜愛的城市品質           Hang Seng Index of Hong Kong as a constituent stock on
      生活服務商」的企業願景，聚焦涵蓋商業運營、           18 November 2022. With strategic positioning of “urban
      物業服務、大會員體系的「2+1」業務模式，致力         quality life service platform”, the Company upholds to its
      打造全業態、全客戶、全產品、全服務一體化生           corporate vision to “become a trustworthy and popular
      態體系。                            urban quality-life service provider among customers”,
                                      focuses on the “2+1” business model covering commercial
                                      operation, property service and membership program, and
                                      strives to create an integrated ecosystem featured with
                                      multiple property types, all customers and full spectrum of
                                      product and service offerings.


      「萬象商業」擁有全國領先的購物中心開發運營           After 19 years of development, “Mixc Commerce” has
      能力，歷經 19 年的發展，從萬象城到萬象匯，         leading development and operation capabilities of
      再到萬象天地，提供全流程服務，即投資策劃、           shopping malls in China from MIXC to MIXC ONE and
      產品定位、設計諮詢、品牌招商、運營服務及其           then to MIXC World, providing whole process services
      他單項或多項服務組合。截至2023 年底，華潤         covering investment planning, product positioning, design
      萬象生活全國在營購物中心101座，其中82個項         consulting, brand tenant sourcing, operation services and
      目零售額排名當地市場前三，零售額達1,812 億        other single or multiple service portfolios. As of the end
      元，會員人數突破4,600萬。                 of 2023, CR Mixc Lifestyle had 101 shopping malls under
                                      operation in China, of which 82 projects ranked the top
                                      three in terms of retail sales in the local market, the retail
                                      sales amounted to RMB181.2 billion, and the number of
                                      members exceeded 46 million.


      「萬象服務」定位城市空間運營服務商，聚焦社區          Positioned as an urban space operational service provider,
      生活空間、辦公生產空間、城市公共空間共 12          “MIXC Services” focuses on 12 types of core scenarios
      類核心場景，通過城市生態圈一體化運營與服            including community living space, office production space
      務，運用科技智慧平台，整合城市資源、運營城           and urban public space, to integrate urban resources,
      市空間、服務城市人民，為人們帶來更非凡的城           operate urban space and serve urban people through
      市生活體驗。截至2023 年底，萬象服務佈局全         integrated operation and service of urban ecosystem as
      國171個城市，在管面積為3.70億平方米，合約        well as technology and intelligent platforms, so as to bring
      面積為4.25億平方米。                    people more extraordinary urban life experience. As of the
                                      end of 2023, MIXC Services covered 171 cities in China
                                      with area under management of 370 million sq.m. and
                                      contracted area of 425 million sq.m.

      主席報告
      CHAIRMAN’
              S STATEMENT
      本人欣然向各位股東提呈本集團截至二零二三年           I am pleased to present to Shareholders the business
      十二月三十一日止之年度業績回顧與展望。             review and outlook of the Group for the year ended 31
                                      December 2023.

      二零二三年，中國經濟回升向好，高質量發展扎           I n 2023, C h i n a ’ s e c o n o m y r e b o u n d e d w i t h s t a b l e
      實推進。中國政府高度重視在穩增長中擴大國            high-quality development. The Chinese government highly
      內需求，把恢復和擴大消費擺在優先位置，通過           underscores the increase of domestic demand while
      多渠道增加居民收入、改善消費環境、提振消費           stabilising growth, and gave priority to restoring and
      信心，有力促進消費持續恢復向好，消費規模再           expanding consumption. Various efforts to increase the
      創新高。與此同時，居民消費呈現出加速分層的           income of residents, improve the consumption environment

由於本集團業務主要於中國內地進行，本集團主         As the Group’s business is mainly conducted in Chinese
要採用人民幣作為結算貨幣。截至二零二三年十         Mainland, we mainly adopt RMB as the settlement
二月三十一日止，非人民幣資產及負債主要為現         currency. As of 31 December 2023, non RMB assets and
金港幣74.0百萬元及19,358.0美元。管理層認為   liabilities mainly included the cash of HK$74.0 million and
本集團在經營方面並無重大外匯風險，人民幣匯         US$19,358.0. The management believed that the operation
率波動不會對本集團財務狀況帶來顯著影響。本         of the Group was not exposed to material foreign currency
集團目前並無外幣風險對沖政策，然而，管理層         risk. No significant impact was caused by the fluctuation
將對外匯風險敞口實施動態監控並將根據市場環         of RMB exchange rate on the Group’s financial position.
境的變化進行必要調整。                   Currently, the Group does not have any hedging policies

                           management of the Group.

      架。此框架列出合適及時地處理及發放內幕消息           Disclosure of Inside Information issued by the Securities
      的程序及內部控制，例如下列步驟：確定充足詳           and Futures Commission. The framework sets out the
      情、對事宜及其對本公司可能產生的影響進行內           procedures and internal controls for the handling and
      部評估，在需要時尋求專業意見，並核實事實。           dissemination of inside information in an appropriate
      向公眾全面披露資料前，知悉有關消息的任何人           and timely manner, such as steps to ascertain sufficient
      士必須確定嚴守保密，不得買賣本公司任何證            details, conduct internal assessment of the matter and its
      券。                              likely impact on the Company, seek professional advice

道之一。本公司確保股東的意見能向董事會反    of the principal channels for communicating with the
映。於股東週年大會席上，包括選舉個別董事在   Shareholders. The Company ensures that Shareholders’

主要風險及不確定性               PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES
本集團的財務狀況、經營業績、業務及前景受到   The Group’s financial condition, results of operations,
諸多風險及不確定性的影響。本集團已識別的主   businesses and prospects may be affected by a number
要風險及不確定性如下：             of risks and uncertainties. The key risks and uncertainties
                        identified by the Group are set out as follows:


物業管理及商業運營服務市場的增長視       The growth of property management and
乎整體經濟及市場狀況              commercial operation service markets

與持份者的關係                 RELATIONSHIP WITH STAKEHOLDERS
本集團深明僱員、客戶及業務夥伴是其可持續發   The Group recognises that employees, customers and
展的關鍵。本集團致力與僱員建立密切及關懷的   business partners are keys to its sustainable development.
關係，為客戶提供優質服務，並加強與其業務夥   The Group is committed to establishing a close and
伴的合作。本公司為員工提供公平及安全的工作   caring relationship with its employees, providing quality
環境，促進員工多元化發展，並基於彼等的優點   services to its customers and enhancing cooperation
及表現提供具競爭力的薪酬及福利以及職業發展   with its business partners. The Company provides a
機會。本集團亦持續為僱員提供充分的培訓及發   fair and safe workplace, promotes diversity to its staff,
展資源，讓彼等了解最新市場及行業發展資訊，   provides competitive remuneration and benefits and
同時提高彼等的表現及在崗位中實現自我發展。   career development opportunities based on their merits
有關本集團僱員的進一步詳情載於本報告「人力   and performance. The Group also puts ongoing efforts to
資源」一段。                  provide adequate trainings and development resources to
                        its employees so that they can keep abreast of the latest
                        development of the market and the industry and, at the
                        same time, improve their performance and self-fulfillment
                        in their positions. Further details in relation to the Group’s
                        employees are set out in the paragraph headed “Human
                        Resources” in this report.


本集團深明與客戶保持良好關係的重要性，為客   The Group understands that it is important to maintain
戶提供滿足彼等需要及要求的服務。本集團透過   good relationship with customers and provide the services
與客戶持續互動以洞悉不同服務日益轉變的市場   in a way that satisfies needs and requirements of the
需求，使本集團能夠積極回應市場需要，藉此增   customers. The Group enhances the relationship by
強彼此關係。本集團亦設立程序處理客戶投訴，   continuous interaction with customers to gain insight on
確保及時迅速解決客戶投訴。           the changing market demand for the services so that
                        the Group can respond proactively. The Group has also

INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’
                   S REPORT


致華潤萬象生活有限公司股東的獨立核數師報告      I N D E P E N D E N T A U D I T O R ’S R E P O R T T O T H E
書                          SHAREHOLDERS OF CHINA RESOURCES MIXC
                           LIFESTYLE SERVICES LIMITED
（於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公司）           (Incorporated in the Cayman Islands with limited liability)

意見                         OPINION
本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審核列載於第152   We have audited the consolidated financial statements
頁至第315頁華潤萬象生活有限公司（「貴公司」）   of China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited (the
及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）的綜合財務     “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) set out
報表，此綜合財務報表包括於二零二三年十二月      on pages 152 to 315, which comprise the consolidated
三十一日的綜合財務狀況表，以及截至該日止年      statement of financial position as at 31 December 2023,
度的綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權益變動      the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other
表及綜合現金流量表以及附註，包括重大會計政      comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of
策資料及其他解釋信息。                changes in equity and the consolidated statement cash
                           flows for the year then ended and notes, comprising
                           material accounting policy information and other
                           explanatory information.


我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計       In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements

                          FOR THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL
                          STATEMENTS
貴公司董事須負責根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香     The directors of the Company are responsible for the
港財務報告準則及香港公司條例的披露規定編製     preparation of the consolidated financial statements that
真實而中肯的綜合財務報表，並對董事認為為使     give a true and fair view in accordance with HKFRSs
綜合財務報表的編製不存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導     issued by the HKICPA and the disclosure requirements of
致的重大錯誤陳述所需的內部控制負責。        the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance and for such internal
                          control as the directors determine is necessary to enable
                          the preparation of consolidated financial statements that
                          are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud
                          or error.


在編製綜合財務報表時，董事負責評估 貴集團     In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the
持續經營的能力，並在適用情況下披露與持續經     directors are responsible for assessing the Group’s ability
營有關的事項，以及使用持續經營為會計基礎，     to continue as a going concern, disclosing, as applicable,
除非董事有意將 貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別     matters related to going concern and using the going
無其他實際的替代方案。               concern basis of accounting unless the directors either
                          intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations or
                          have no realistic alternative but to do so.

      他目的。我們概不就本報告的內容對任何其他人           opinion. This report is made solely to you, as a body, and

                                      towards or accept liability to any other person for the

      審計準則進行的審核總能發現所存在的重大錯誤           is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance
      陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合           with HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement

          合營企業為本集團或本公司擁有                 and operating policies. A joint venture is
          共同控制權的安排，當中本集團或                an arrangement in which the Group or the
          本公司擁有對該安排資產淨值的權                Company has joint control, whereby the
          利，而非對其資產的權利及負債的                Group or the Company has the rights to the
          義務。                            net assets of the arrangement, rather than
                                         rights to its assets and obligations for its
                                         liabilities.


          於一間聯營公司或一間合營企業之                An interest in an associate or a joint venture
          權益採用權益法入賬。有關權益初                is accounted for using the equity method.
          步按成本（包括交易成本）確認。                They are initially recognised at cost, which
          其後，綜合財務報表包括本集團應                includes transaction costs. Subsequently,
          佔該等投資對象的損益及其他全面                the consolidated financial statements include
          收益（「其他全面收益」），直至重               the Group’s share of the profit or loss and
          大影響力或共同控制權終止當日為                other comprehensive income (“OCI”) of those
          止。                             investees, until the date on which significant
                                         influence or joint control ceases.

                預期信貸虧損的計量                         Measurement of ECLs

                預期信貸虧損為按概率加                       ECLs are a probability-weighted
                權估計的信貸虧損。一般                       estimate of credit losses. Generally,
                而言，信貸虧損以合同及                       credit losses are measured as the
                預期金額之間的所有預期                       present value of all expected cash
                現金差額的現值計量。                        shortfalls between the contractual and
                                                  expected amounts.

                倘貼現影響重大，則預期                       The expected cash shortfalls are
                現金差額將採用以下利率                       discounted using the following rates if
                貼現：                               the effect is material:

                －   固定利率金融資                       –       fixed-rate financial assets,
                    產、貿易及其他應                              trade and other receivables
                    收款項及合同資                               and contract assets: effective
                    產：於初始確認時                              interest rate determined at initial
                    釐定的實際利率或                              recognition or an approximation
                    其近似值；及                                thereof; and

                －   浮息金融資產：即                      –       variable-rate financial assets:
                    期實際利率。                                current effective interest rate.

                估計預期信貸虧損時所考                       The maximum period considered
                慮的最長期間是以本集團                       when estimating ECLs is the maximum
                面對信貸風險的最長合同                       contractual period over which the
                期間為準。                             Group is exposed to credit risk.

    (t)   撥備及或然負債                 (t)    Provisions and contingent
                                         liabilities
          一般而言，撥備乃按反映當時市場                Generally provisions are determined by
          對貨幣時間價值及負債特定風險評                discounting the expected future cash flows
          估之稅前利率貼現預期未來現金流                at a pre-tax rate that reflects current market
          量釐定。                           assessment of the time value of money and
                                         the risks specific to the liability.


          保證撥備於相關產品或服務已售出                A provision for warranties is recognised when
          時基於過往保證數據及權衡可能後                the underlying products or services are sold,
          果與相關概率確認。                      based on historical warranty data and a
                                         weighting of possible outcomes against their
                                         associated probabilities.


          虧損性合同之撥備乃按終止合同預                A provision for onerous contracts is measured
          期成本及繼續合同預期成本淨額                 at the present value of the lower of the
          （以較低者為準）的現值計量，現                expected cost of terminating the contract and
          值乃根據履行合同項下責任的增量                the expected net cost of continuing with the
          成本及履行合同直接相關的其他成                contract, which is determined based on the
          本分配釐定。於計提撥備前，本集                incremental costs of fulfilling the obligation
          團確認該合同有關資產之任何減值                under that contract and an allocation of other
          虧損（見附註2(k)(ii)）。               costs directly related to fulfilling that contract.
                                         Before a provision is established, the Group
                                         recognises any impairment loss on the assets
                                         associated with that contract (see note 2(k)
                                         (ii)).


          倘不太可能需要經濟利益流出，或                Where it is not probable that an outflow
          無法對有關數額作出可靠估計，則                of economic benefits will be required,
          會將該責任披露為或然負債，除非                or the amount cannot be estimated
          經濟利益流出的可能性極微。其存                reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a
          在僅能以一宗或以上未來事件的發                contingent liability, unless the probability
          生與否來確定的潛在責任，除非經                of outflow of economic benefits is remote.
          濟利益流出的可能性極微，否則亦                Possible obligations, whose existence will
          披露為或然負債。                       only be confirmed by the occurrence or
                                         nonoccurrence of one or more future events
                                         are also disclosed as contingent liabilities
                                         unless the probability of outflow of economic
                                         benefits is remote.

                    及負債按交易當日的匯率換算。外

                                                                      rate when the fair value was determined.

                  值測試。當資產或現金產                                there are indicators that the carrying

                  收回金額（即公允價值減出                               impairment exists when the carrying
                  售成本與使用價值兩者中                                value of an asset or a cash-generating
                  的 較 高 者 ）時 ， 即 存 在 減                       unit exceeds its recoverable amount,
                  值。公允價值減出售成本                                which is the higher of its fair value less
                  的計算基於類似資產的公                                costs of disposal and its value in use.
                  平交易中具有約束力的銷                                The calculation of the fair value less
                  售交易的現有數據，或可                                costs of disposal is based on available
                  觀察市場價格減去出售資                                data from binding sales transactions
                  產的增量成本。進行使用                                i n an arm ’ s l ength transacti on of
                  價值計算時，管理層必須                                similar assets or observable market
                  估計資產或現金產生單位                                prices less incremental costs for
                  的預計未來現金流量，並                                disposing of the asset. When value-
                  選擇適當的折現率，以計                                in-use calculations are undertaken,
                  算該等現金流量的現值。                                management must estimate the
                                                             expected future cash flows from the

                    本集團投資物業位於中國內地。本集                                      The Group’s investment properties are situated
                    團按公允價值計量其投資物業。本集                                      in Chinese Mainland. The Group measures its
                    團投資物業於二零二三年十二月三十                                      investment properties at fair value. The fair value
                    一日的公允價值乃基於獨立專業合資                                      of the Group’s investment properties as at 31
                    格估值師仲量聯行企業評估及諮詢有                                      December 2023 have been determined on the basis
                    限公司（「仲量聯行」）進行的估值釐定。                                   of valuation carried out by Jones Lang LaSalle
                                                                          Corporate Appraisal and Advisory Limited (“JLL”),
                                                                          an independent and professionally qualified valuer.


              (b)   估值技術                                         (b)      Valuation techniques


                    估值中採用的估值方法為收入法及剩                                      The valuation methodology adopted in valuation are
                    餘法。                                                   the income approach and residual method.


                    收入法 － 收入法的運作考慮來自現有                                    Income approach – the income approach operates
                    租賃的物業租賃收入（就潛在的租賃復                                     by considering the rental income of the property
                    歸收入作出適當撥備），然後以適當的                                     derived from the existing tenancy with due
                    資本化率資本化為價值。                                           allowance for the reversionary income potential of
                                                                          the tenancy, which is then capitalised into the value
                                                                          at an appropriate capitalisation rate.


                    剩餘法 － 剩餘法考慮總發展價值（「總                                   Residual method – the residual approach takes
                    發 展 價 值 」）並 扣 除 尚 未 發 生 的 建 築                         into account the residual value of the gross
                    成本、開支以及發展利潤後的剩餘價                                      development value (“GDV”) after deduction of
                    值。剩餘法首先計算假設建議發展項                                      the outstanding construction costs and expenses
                    目於估值日已落成的合計總發展價                                       as well as profit element. It involves firstly the
                    值。建議發展項目的估計成本包括建                                      assessment of the aggregate GDV of the proposed
                    築成本、市場推廣支出、專業費用、                                      development, as if completed, as at the valuation
                    財務費用以及相關成本，另加發展商                                      date. Estimated costs of the proposed development
                    的風險及利潤撥備。尚未發生的開發                                      include construction costs, marketing expense,
                    成本將由總發展價值中扣除，並得出                                      professional fee, finance charges and associated
                    標的物業的剩餘價值。                                            costs, plus an allowance for the developer’s risk
                                                                          and profit. The outstanding development costs
                                                                          are then deducted from the GDV and the resultant
                                                                          figure is the residual value of the subject property.

                                                                              under common control.

                           註冊資本的50%為止。轉                                       of its profit after tax, as determined

     (d)   資本管理                             (d)    Capital management
           本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團                         The Group manages its capital to ensure that
           內實體有能力進行持續經營，並透                         entities in the Group will be able to continue
           過優化債務與權益平衡為股東帶來                         as a going concern while maximising
           最大回報。                                   the return to shareholders through the
                                                   optimisation of the debt and equity balance.


           本公司董事持續檢討負債與資產比                         The directors of the Company review the
           率（為總負債除以總資產），計及                         liability-to-asset ratio, which is total liabilities
           資本成本及與各類資本相關的風                          divided by total assets, on a continuous
           險。本集團將透過籌集新債務及贖                         basis, taking into account the cost of capital
           回現有債務而平衡整體資本架構，                         and the risks associated with each class of
           並管理負債與資產比率。本集團的                         capital. The Group will balance its overall
           整體策略於年內維持不變。                            capital structure through the raising of new
                                                   debts as well as the redemption of the
                                                   existing debts, and manage the liability to
                                                   asset ratio. The Group’s overall strategy
                                                   remained unchanged during the year.

       30     業務合併                                          30   BUSINESS COMBINATION
              收購成都市朗基生活服務有限公                                     Acquisition of Chengdu Langji Life
              司                                                  Service Co., Ltd
              於二零二三年十月，本集團向第三方收購                                 On October 2023, the Group acquired a 100%
              成都市朗基生活服務有限公司（「朗基」）                                equity interest in Chengdu Langji Life Service Co.,
              100%權益，代價包括現金代價及按受管                                Ltd (“Langji”) from a third party at a consideration
              面積實際交付進度計量的或然代價。朗基                                 including cash consideration and contingent
              從事物業管理業務。收購事項為本集團於                                 consideration at fair value based on the actual
              中國內地擴展其物業管理業務策略之其中                                 delivery progress of the managed areas. Langji is
              一環而作出。                                             engaged in the property management service. The
                                                                 acquisition was made as part of the Group’s strategy
                                                                 to expand its property management operation in the
                                                                 Chinese Mainland.

       31     金融風險管理及金融工具的公                                 31   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT
              允價值                                                AND FAIR VALUES OF FINANCIAL
                                                                 INSTRUMENTS
              本集團於日常業務過程中會產生信貸、流                                 Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and
              動資金、利率及貨幣風險。本集團亦因其                                 currency risks arises in the normal course of the
              於其他實體的股本投資及其本身股份價格                                 Group’s business. The Group is also exposed to
              變動而面臨股本價格風險。                                       equity price risk arising from its equity investments
                                                                 in other entities and movements in its own equity
                                                                 share price.


              本集團所面對的該等風險及本集團用以管                                 The Group’s exposure to these risks and the
              理該等風險的金融風險管理政策及慣例載                                 financial risk management policies and practices
              述如下。                                               used by the Group to manage these risks are
                                                                 described below.

              (a)   信貸風險                                         (a)   Credit risk
                    本集團就其貿易應收款項及應收票                                    The Group is exposed to credit risk in relation
                    據、其他應收款項、現金及現金等                                    to its trade and bill receivables and other
                    價物及受限制銀行存款面臨信貸風                                    receivables, cash and cash equivalents and
                    險。                                                 restricted bank deposits.


                    本集團預期並無與現金及現金等價                                    The Group expects that there is no significant
                    物以及受限制銀行存款相關的重                                     credit risk associated with cash and cash
                    大信貸風險，因為有關款項主要存                                    equivalents and restricted bank deposits
                    放於國有銀行及其他大中型上市銀                                    since they are substantially deposited at
                    行。管理層預期將不會因該等交易                                    state-owned banks and other medium or
                    對手不履約而蒙受任何重大損失。                                    large-sized listed banks. Management does
                                                                       not expect that there will be any significant
                                                                       losses from non-performance by these
                                                                       counterparties.


                    本集團預期與貿易應收款項及應收                                    The Group expects that the credit risk
                    票據以及應收關聯方的其他應收款                                    associated with trade and bill receivables and
                    項有關的信貸風險較低，因為關聯                                    other receivables due from related parties
                    方有足夠的能力於短期內履行合同                                    to be low, since the related parties have
                    現金流量義務。                                            strong capacity to meet contractual cash flow
                                                                       obligations in the near term.
